# AI Media Scout — 2026-11-19

This edition provides 76 complete documentation guides and 6 additional screened leads. 16 earlier screened entries received full guides. New and updated records carry their own dated evidence checks. This edition looks at archival film and recorded-audio work, camera-driven projection, editable digital publishing, and handoffs for motion, captions and creative production. Software licensing is reviewed independently of model terms, any paid service or required proprietary host. Documentation and published examples informed the quality checks. No discovered app was installed or run. The frozen plan covered 112 fields with 1264/1264 repository queries attempted. 1236 completed successfully, 2 were partial, and 26 failed. 233 were bounded. All 31 configured model tasks were attempted. 0 model tasks failed. The collection retained explicit warnings and limits. Metadata screening indexed all 2,601 saved repository pages, spanning 80,581 numeric identities. The complete retained backlog was consulted alongside today's metadata, including earlier pilot candidates. It currently has 115,508 raw records and 1,142 previously screened pending guides. A metadata signal does not establish software eligibility. Unreviewed and excluded candidates remain in private research records. Live primary research beyond GitHub covered all observed fields and the configured ecosystems. The notes identify limits such as access and rate restrictions, failed fetches and bounded discovery pages. The counts represent the documented searches and available pages. Evidence label counts were 0 Recommended, 1 Ready to try, 76 Quality unverified, 5 Experimental. Beat This! meets the Ready to try criteria for the stated scope. Beat This! 1.1.0, default final0 model, offline beat and downbeat markers from audio using a local CPU-supported PyTorch environment. Credential-pattern material in unsealed source captures was redacted before sealing. The original and redacted content hashes and provenance stay in the local evidence record. 30 model tasks reached the configured maximum of 50 returned rows each. The model listings are bounded discovery leads. Model-task responses with no rows: text2text-generation. The separate watchlist and evidence enrichment recorded 62 individual watchlist failures and 4 evidence failures. Source failures are retained as gaps.

Documentation reviewed today. Tools are not hands-on tested unless explicitly stated. Requirements and performance remain source-specific.

## Coverage

| 3D, reconstruction & assets | SolidCraft documents editable parametric sketches and an optional agent connection. Shape Studio separately documents local image-to-GLB or OBJ inference. Mesh export alone gives no evidence of watertightness or production print tolerances. Venue network stability and platform support must be assessed per tool. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. | Performance, projection & stage media | Koaia documents AI effects in video feeds and masks. Ossia documents live mapping and time-based control. Evidence for a specific performance needs the actual build, models and latency path. [Source 1](https://ossia.io/projection-mapping.html) [Source 2](https://suite.sat.qc.ca/en/tools/) [Source 3](https://sat-mtl.github.io/koaia/) [Source 4](https://sat-mtl.github.io/koaia/installation.html) [Source 5](https://sat-mtl.github.io/livepose/installation.html) [Source 6](https://ossia.io/generative-art.html) [Source 7](https://ossia.io/score-docs/examples/ai) |
| AI photobook, album sequencing and editorial layout | The visual cover study connects generated art to music metadata. It does not verify editable book spreads, pagination or printer-ready attachments. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.01651) |
| Photography, restoration & color | The photo-preservation and ATune sources describe learned restoration and editable slider-based retouching. Preserve original tones and inspect color-result claims against the workflow assessed. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22291) [Source 2](https://huggingface.co/tasks/image-classification) [Source 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16236) [Source 4](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.16236v1) [Source 5](https://autoshade.dev/) |
| Archives, media restoration & collections | PyPottery and the model-task descriptions show derived traces, OCR and captions. Verify originals and retention separately from model-generated derivatives. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://lrncrd.github.io/PyPottery/) [Source 2](https://huggingface.co/tasks/feature-extraction) [Source 3](https://huggingface.co/tasks/image-to-text) [Source 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source 5](https://www.archives.gov/preservation/formats/motion-picture-film.html) |
| AI print separation and prepress authoring | Imprimatur's relief roller preview is useful context. It provides no proof of ordinary press imposition, bleed, color management or an open learned process from this pass. [Source 1](https://www.imprimatur.fyi/) |
| Neural watermarking for printed creative media | StegaStamp describes hiding information in imagery intended for digital display or printing. Paper, capture and lighting conditions matter when evaluating whether the decoded mark survives. [Source 1](https://www.matthewtancik.com/stegastamp) |
| AI printmaking, risograph and editioned art | The Imprimatur site documents generating printable roller geometry. The accessible source does not establish an AI-specific open implementation or adequate pressure and registration on press. [Source 1](https://www.imprimatur.fyi/) |
| Neural procedural material authoring | VideoNeuMat documents learned material extraction from generative video. W3C WebGPU specifies a compute interface. Neither guarantees a reusable material graph for a target DCC application. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. 1 source response reached the size bound. [Source 1](https://bowenxueai.github.io/VideoNeuMat/) [Source 2](https://www.w3.org/TR/webgpu/) [Source 3](https://registry.npmjs.org/three-ntc/latest) [Source 4](https://registry.npmjs.org/three-ntc-trainer/latest) [Source 5](https://research.nvidia.com/labs/rtr/neural_texture_compression/) [Source 6](https://www.npmjs.com/package/neural-texture-engine) |
| Creative publishing & presentation | The Manimvox project page shows developer-provided animation examples. Some publisher pages were unavailable. Uploaded source material, generated clips and export behavior must be checked per tool. 2 archived source attempts failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://laplacianai.github.io/manimvox/) [Source 2](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Piangpi1997/PDF-translator-Ai/main/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://openslides.dev/) [Source 4](https://manimvox.laplacian.ai/) |
| Neural rigging and digital puppetry | The animation paper learns new character motions from example images. Keypoint task documentation clarifies a separate pose output. Neither by itself gives verified stage control. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/1910.02060) [Source 2](https://huggingface.co/tasks/keypoint-detection) |
| Neural relighting and material recovery | AEGIR estimates area illumination from observed views. Sapiens normal predictions are separate inputs for compositing. A depth or normal map is not a tested relit plate. [Source 1](https://darkgeekms.github.io/projects/aegir/) [Source 2](https://huggingface.co/facebook/sapiens2-normal-1b) |
| Neural denoising for real-time creative rendering | Open Image Denoise identifies a ray-tracing denoiser. Blender's current sampling guide gives host context. The renderer, pass inputs and hardware path determine real suitability. [Source 1](https://www.openimagedenoise.org/) [Source 2](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/render/cycles/render_settings/sampling.html) |
| Neural rhythm tracking and synchronized visual media | The rhythm research distinguishes downbeat and meter tracking from tempo alone. BeatNet-RS documentation describes a local inference component. Synchronization to an output still depends on buffering and device latency. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2108.03576) [Source 2](https://docs.rs/crate/beatnet-rs/latest/source/README.md) [Source 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21658) [Source 4](https://cloud.cp.jku.at/index.php/s/7ik4RrBKTS273gp) [Source 5](https://github.com/CPJKU/beat_this/releases/tag/v1.1.0) [Source 6](https://github.com/OakChaser/beat-visualizer) [Source 7](https://github.com/OakChaser/beat-visualizer/releases/latest) [Source 8](https://huggingface.co/6l3m/kairos-music/tree/main) |
| AI robotic painting and drawing | Form's authors study using new deformable materials in robotic manipulation. The result applies to the tested material/rig conditions and does not establish desktop deployability. [Source 1](https://form-robots.github.io/) |
| Learned calligraphy and robotic brush performance | CalliRewrite documents motion planning and manipulation for strokes. Inspect the actual arm control prerequisites and the output at the chosen ink, scale and paper. [Source 1](https://luoprojectpage.github.io/callirewrite/) |
| Learned robotic shadow performance and inverse silhouette control | Duke's work concerns learned robotic shadow silhouettes. CUNY and Sunway document separate puppet preservation and performance research. None of those contexts by itself clears transferable production code. [Source 1](https://academicworks.cuny.edu/ny_pubs/1252/) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22434) [Source 3](https://sunwayuniversity.edu.my/research/centres/SCDHCH/woyae-tangae) [Source 4](https://generalroboticslab.com/shadow) |
| AI seamless loops, sample design and DAW-ready stems | WaivePulse describes an offline suite. A captured product page does not establish every underlying model grant or seamless playback at chosen tempos. [Source 1](https://waivepulse.com/) |
| AI semantic editing of captured 3D scenes | Semantic Gaussians links foundation-model features to editable 3D selections. One Feature 3DGS path was unavailable. Export and reliable masking need verification in the individual package. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://semantic-gaussians.github.io/) [Source 2](https://sharinka0715.github.io/semantic-gaussians/) [Source 3](https://shijiezhou-ucla.github.io/feature-3dgs/) |
| AI shadow puppetry and hand-shadow performance | Research and performance sources distinguish tracked hands, a procedural shadow scene and digital preservation of traditional puppetry. An OpenReview response presented a browser verification screen and several other attempts failed. 3 archived source attempts failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://academicworks.cuny.edu/ny_pubs/1252/) [Source 2](https://sketchry.app/skills/shadowplay/) [Source 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10360) [Source 4](https://www.ewadirect.com/proceedings/ace/article/view/15328) [Source 5](https://openreview.net/forum?id=77b0dc11d1a9b57b3baf4b168cc2f00a42278f67) [Source 6](https://aaltodoc.aalto.fi/bitstreams/81cba736-c8ed-4d5b-bb24-e99bdce504ea/download) [Source 7](https://research.google/blog/shadowplay-using-our-hands-to-have-some-fun-with-ai/) |
| AI signing avatars and expressive gesture media | PHOSA documents sign-avatar modeling. Accuracy of signing, facial expression and how deaf users assess delivered material remain distinct from photorealism. [Source 1](https://naaapi.github.io/PHOSA/) |
| AI assisted sound data art and corpus instruments | Parallax documents a maker's media work combining neural imagery and sonification. It does not establish a standalone licensed tool for repeating the process. [Source 1](https://www.sonify.io/parallax-ai-art/) |
| Neural soundscape ecology and sonic art | SoundPlot describes neural audio synthesis with acoustic feature analysis. An artistic projection of embeddings must not be treated as verified species attribution. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12752) |
| Spatial audio & volumetric media | Current neural acoustic fields research explores sound with representation of physical space. Playback on a specific speaker or headset array remains a separate check. 1 source response reached the size bound. [Source 1](https://seexpeng.github.io/data/papers/Neural%20Acoustic%20Fields%20for%20Mixed%20Reality.pdf) [Source 2](https://par.nsf.gov/biblio/10647880) [Source 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.14342) [Source 4](https://soundspaces.org/) |
| AI stage lighting and show control | Ossia and QLC+ document timeline/control options. The camera model's detections and the behavior of a real console or fixture are distinct verification tasks. [Source 1](https://ossia.io/projection-mapping.html) [Source 2](https://lacylights.com/) [Source 3](https://qlcplus.org/) [Source 4](https://suite.sat.qc.ca/en/tools/) [Source 5](https://sat-mtl.github.io/koaia/) [Source 6](https://sat-mtl.github.io/koaia/installation.html) [Source 7](https://sat-mtl.github.io/livepose/installation.html) [Source 8](https://ossia.io/generative-art.html) |
| AI stereo conversion and spatial video authoring | The stereo style-transfer paper treats the left and right views together. Test corresponding edges and viewing comfort before considering a result suitable for exhibition. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.10591) |
| AI-assisted stop motion and frame animation | Eagle Animation supplies direct capture and editing context. Reviewed documentation does not by itself establish a learned cleanup or in-between feature. [Source 1](https://eagle-animation.com/) |
| Storyboarding, narrative & comics | The dramai source describes a local short-drama studio as an orchestration product. One documentation route failed. Review provider calls, sequence exports and rights individually. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://hyyyyyyz.github.io/dramai/) [Source 2](https://hyyyyyyz.github.io/dramai/docs/) |
| AI pattern transcription and symbolic craft verification | A Qwen model listing and craft workflow documentation provide distinct evidence. A model answer cannot establish the physical correctness of embroidery, patterns or fabrication instructions. [Source 1](https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B) [Source 2](https://stitchfoundry.github.io/StitchFoundry/) |
| AI tactile graphics and accessible relief media | Current tactile generation studies and AltCanvas illustrate prompt-guided image creation. Tactile standards still require clarity, distinguishable features and a physical or user review. SVG export alone does not meet that bar. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22674) [Source 2](https://shljessie.github.io/AltCanvas/) [Source 3](https://www.brailleauthority.org/tg/) [Source 4](https://www.perkins.org/resource/creating-tactile-graphics/) [Source 5](https://ruihangao.github.io/Text2TactileGraphics/) [Source 6](https://cs224r.stanford.edu/projects/pdfs/CS_224R_Final_Project__1_.pdf) |
| Typography, fonts & layout | The VecGlypher documentation describes a model that emits SVG glyph outlines from text or references. A complete font needs broader glyph coverage, metrics and licensing checks. The source data notes identify Envato materials that were not included in the release. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://xk-huang.github.io/VecGlypher/assets/VecGlypher.pdf) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21461) [Source 3](https://xk-huang.github.io/VecGlypher/) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/papers/2602.21461) [Source 5](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCE-Taiwan71481.2026) [Source 6](https://sushihex.github.io/digital-rain/) |
| Neural underwater photography, film and scene restoration | UDnet's authors describe neural restoration for green/blue cast and haze. Documented results should be interpreted for the actual water, lighting and camera conditions. [Source 1](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/) |
| Vector graphics, illustration & textures | The SVGRender account describes learned vector rendering on the Hub. A separate vector file still needs review for editable paths, transforms and usable layer structure. [Source 1](https://huggingface.co/SVGRender) |
| VFX, compositing & relighting | The Light-Aware Compositing paper was available as a primary PDF. The particular software grant and integration in a live production pipeline were not established from that source alone. [Source 1](https://yaksoy.github.io/papers/SIG25p-LightAwareCompositing.pdf) [Source 2](https://gruvi.cs.sfu.ca/publications/) [Source 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.03698) |
| Video, animation & film | OpenReelio, Kaestral and Phosphene materials document different editable-timeline and generation routes. Read model and platform dependencies per path. Draft previews are not proof of repeatable export. [Source 1](https://openreelio.com/) [Source 2](https://www.kaestral.com/) [Source 3](https://phosphene.video/changelog/) [Source 4](https://koracut.org/en/docs/start/) [Source 5](https://koracut.org/en/) [Source 6](https://koracut.org/en/docs/drive/) |
| AI visual dubbing and translated performance | X-Dub's developer examples report long lip-sync sequences. The clips do not establish independent performance, rights to voices or a complete reusable software package. [Source 1](https://hjrphoebus.github.io/X-Dub/) |
| Data art & scientific visualization | The AI+VIS survey describes modeling and visual analytics research. It provides discovery context and does not verify a current source grant or accessibility of a specific authoring app. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.06504) |
| Browser tools & web media | The browser LLM study and WebGPU documentation describe compute options. Hardware adapters, browser versions and cached weights affect any particular local creative workflow. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15803) [Source 2](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/WebGPU_API) |
| AI furniture, timber and joinery design | ShopPrentice presents a furniture design tool with agent input. The companion paper route was unavailable. Verify units, stock strength and cut-list accuracy outside the generated view. 1 archived source attempt failed or returned an access challenge. [Source 1](https://shopprentice.com/) [Source 2](https://www.mdpi.com/2076-3417/14/7/3011) |
| WebXR, VR & AR | Meta's IWSDK and the Three-Live project show agent and browser creation routes. Headset, feature and browser support still need verification for the intended scene. [Source 1](https://developers.meta.com/vr/blog/accelerate-vr-development-with-ai-and-immersive-web-sdk/) [Source 2](https://opensource.googleblog.com/2026/11/introducing-three-live-ai-generated-3d-ar-webxr.html) |

## Search and review counts

112 creative fields; 1264/1264 repository queries attempted; 31/31 model-task queries attempted. 81352 distinct source candidates and 1377 model leads. 76 detailed profiles and 6 additional screened discoveries. Source gaps: 26 failed repository queries, 2 partial repository queries, 233 bounded repository queries, 0 failed model-task queries and 0 web ecosystem gaps. Raw search candidates include duplicates of known tools, excluded projects and projects awaiting review; they are not verified recommendations.

## Research beyond GitHub

- **gitlab** · searched: Topic searches returned production and concept projects. The direct openedit source tree contains callable editor source, its MIT grant and bilingual guides. It remains an existing guide. SAT’s direct GitLab release and full GPLv3 text establish the original LivePose source at 1.12.0. It matches the library’s existing LivePose entry. Other topic matches expose specifications or procedural mapping tools without establishing a released creative AI implementation. Subsequent direct calls were partly rate limited. [Source](https://gitlab.com/explore/projects/topics/ai?language_name=CSS&sort=latest_activity_desc) [Source](https://gitlab.com/explore/projects/topics/video-generation?sort=name_desc) [Source](https://gitlab.com/explore/projects/topics/projection-mapping) [Source](https://gitlab.com/afsh4ck/openedit) [Source](https://gitlab.com/afsh4ck/openedit/-/raw/main/LICENSE) [Source](https://gitlab.com/sat-mtl/tools/livepose/livepose/-/releases/1.12.0) [Source](https://gitlab.com/sat-mtl/tools/livepose/livepose/-/raw/1.12.0/License.md)
- **codeberg** · searched: The four broad Codeberg search APIs for subtitling, diffusion, restoration and segmentation returned HTTP 429. Direct calls still retrieved the Imaginer project and the full Apache text for the separate Raspberry Pi Diffusion repository. These targeted checks do not resolve the broad discovery gap. [Source](https://codeberg.org/api/v1/repos/search?q=subtitle&limit=20) [Source](https://codeberg.org/api/v1/repos/search?q=restoration&limit=20) [Source](https://codeberg.org/api/v1/repos/Imaginer/Imaginer) [Source](https://codeberg.org/MightyOwlbear/Raspberry-Pi-5-Diffusion/raw/branch/main/LICENSE)
- **sourcehut** · searched: Checked projection, transcription, diffusion and sampler project searches. The existing diffuser lead’s published Cargo manifest continues to use a local source path for a core dependency. Performer use, installation portability and comparable coverage of all SourceHut projects remain unresolved. [Source](https://sr.ht/projects?search=projection) [Source](https://sr.ht/projects?search=transcription) [Source](https://sr.ht/projects?search=diffusion) [Source](https://sr.ht/projects?search=sampler) [Source](https://git.sr.ht/~rockerboo/diffuser/blob/main/Cargo.toml)
- **packages** · searched: Registry metadata clarified the canonical source of CutAI/EditStyle, Pandrator and the maintained cutroom package. Kinocut 2.0.1 and Revid 0.1.2 are reproducible package references to compare with source. Two guessed Python names failed. A registry license field alone does not verify an entire grant. [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/cutai/json) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/cutroom/json) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/revid/json) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/pandrator/json) [Source](https://registry.npmjs.org/@mediapipe%2Ftasks-vision/latest)
- **creative-plugins** · searched: Ossia documents a separate ONNX add-on and distinct device/host constraints. ComfyUI documents the general node interface, while TouchDesigner provides classical optical flow and can host a separate learned tracking plug-in. Some catalog or documentation requests did not return usable content. [Source](https://ossia.io/score-docs/reference/add-ons.html) [Source](https://docs.comfy.org/custom-nodes/overview) [Source](https://docs.derivative.ca/Optical_Flow_TOP) [Source](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner) [Source](https://extensions.blender.org/tag/ai/)
- **project-sites** · searched: The current project sites describe device-driven projection, an editor whose agent exchanges structured edit files, and accessible artwork generation. Vendor usage claims, model artifacts, grants and third-party use were individually assessed. Access failures and response bounds remain in the field notes. [Source](https://ossia.io/score-docs/examples/ai) [Source](https://akari.video/en/howto/) [Source](https://video-harness.app/) [Source](https://shljessie.github.io/AltCanvas/) [Source](https://www.archives.gov/preservation/formats)
- **research-code** · searched: Primary sources include early-film restoration, synthetic defect masks and tactile image production. The reusable portion is scoped to the released software for which the full code grant could be confirmed. Other research descriptions remain methodological context. [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21219) [Source](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2025/html/Mao_Making_Old_Film_Great_Again_Degradation-aware_State_Space_Model_for_CVPR_2025_paper.html) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22674)
- **international** · searched: Japanese AKARI material documents editor/agent separation and captions. Spanish-English OpenEdit describes its bilingual timeline. The Chinese drama-studio source supplies additional language context. Access attempts for some institutional sources failed, and individual platform capabilities still require exact documentation. [Source](https://akari.video/) [Source](https://akari.video/en/howto/) [Source](https://gitlab.com/afsh4ck/openedit/-/raw/main/README.md) [Source](https://hyyyyyyz.github.io/dramai/) [Source](https://audiostellar.xyz/)

## Proximity Light

Camera-reactive projection installations · Physical, robotic & kinetic installations · Performance, projection & stage media · Browser tools & web media

This is the first complete guide in the library.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: A single-wall installation where a visitor approaching the camera changes the projected color or casts a moving dark region.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-19. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code. Models, services and assets have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The project supplies a synthetic three-panel frame showing camera input, estimated depth and colored projection. It does not demonstrate alignment, delay or reliability on a live installation. [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Serve the source folder locally, then open its localhost URL in Chrome or Edge. The project documents a PowerShell server as well as a standard Python HTTP server route. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** The current repository publishes the source and instructions. The checked releases endpoint showed no releases, and this review did not establish a maintained deployment or support policy.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** The reviewed browser source names Transformers.js, ONNX Community Depth Anything V2 and a separately downloaded MediaPipe Pose Landmarker. Upstream Depth Anything V2 Small is Apache-2.0. The upstream Base and Large models use CC BY-NC 4.0. Verify the exact ONNX conversion and pose artifact terms for the chosen route. Local app and compute. Model and browser code are fetched from external hosts at first use. No subscription is documented for the default route. [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. Relative monocular depth can shift between frames. The image warp aligns one plane and differs on objects at other distances. Calibration, camera permission and initial CDN/model availability are setup dependencies.

### How it uses AI

Depth Anything V2 predicts relative distance from an ordinary webcam. A separate MediaPipe pose mode follows people with a soft spotlight. The browser shapes and warps each result for a second display. [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

### Introduction

This small installation tool gives a webcam and projector separate capture and output controls in an ordinary browser window. [Source 1](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source 3](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source 4](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source 5](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

### What it is good for

A single-wall installation where a visitor approaching the camera changes the projected color or casts a moving dark region. [Source 1](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source 3](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source 4](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source 5](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

The project supplies a synthetic three-panel frame showing camera input, estimated depth and colored projection. It does not demonstrate alignment, delay or reliability on a live installation. [Source 1](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source 3](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source 4](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source 5](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

### Install

Serve the source folder locally, then open its localhost URL in Chrome or Edge. The project documents a PowerShell server as well as a standard Python HTTP server route. [Source 1](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source 3](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source 4](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source 5](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Serve the source folder locally, then open its localhost URL in Chrome or Edge. The project documents a PowerShell server as well as a standard Python HTTP server route.

```sh
python -m http.server
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source 3](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source 4](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source 5](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

1. Start the camera and select the small depth model. Open the projector window on an extended display.
2. Align the camera crop and corner-pin the projection against a flat surface. Freeze a reference background before introducing a visitor.
3. Try the pose-following mode separately. Inspect lag, light spill and missed people under representative lighting.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/ASAMedia/realtimeprojection/blob/main/app.js) [Source 3](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MediaDevices/getUserMedia) [Source 4](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2#pre-trained-models) [Source 5](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js)

- **Hardware:** Webcam, projector or second display, and a WebGPU-capable setup for the accelerated path. The guide names approximately 50 MB, 195 MB and 669 MB model downloads. Minimum system RAM and VRAM are undocumented.
- **Software:** Recent Chrome or Edge with WebGPU, plus a local HTTP server. WASM is documented as a slower fallback. The core app is HTML and JavaScript.
- **Platforms:** Browser deployment in Chrome or Edge. Windows-specific PowerShell and extended-desktop instructions are documented. Compatibility for each GPU and OS has not been established.

Upstream Depth Anything V2 Small is Apache-2.0. The upstream Base and Large models use CC BY-NC 4.0. Verify the exact ONNX conversion and pose artifact terms for the chosen route.

## AbsoluteDegradation

Archival film restoration and preservation · Archives, media restoration & collections · AI assisted media quality and take selection

This is the first complete guide in the library.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Preparing controlled grayscale training examples and comparing dust and scratch removal across short early-film clips.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-19. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code. Models, services and assets have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The paper and README show clean input, generated grayscale damage and a defect mask. They also report a blinded preference study. The study evaluates the authors’ experiment and does not prove recovery of original image content. [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** The project documents a locked uv environment, Python 3.11–3.13, and a separate step for the blue-noise basis and film textures. Read the dataset and texture terms before downloading inputs. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** The standalone augmentation code has an MIT grant and no learned weight requirement. REDS is separately attributed as CC BY 4.0 by the maintainer. The scanned texture inputs come from separate research material. Their reuse grant is unresolved, and the benchmark also has its own dataset terms. Local compute and storage plus access to chosen datasets. Dataset services and content carry separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. The published benchmark targets grayscale material from early cinema. It does not establish generalization to VHS, modern formats or color repair. This entry covers the standalone pipeline, with no grant asserted for any external restoration implementation or texture archive.

### How it uses AI

The project creates damage masks and paired clean/degraded clips for training film-restoration models. The separate real-film benchmark supports comparison of learned restoration systems. The damage generator itself is a scripted augmentation pipeline. [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Introduction

This technical package produces controlled grayscale comparisons for research into damaged early film. [Source 1](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source 3](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source 4](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source 5](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### What it is good for

Preparing controlled grayscale training examples and comparing dust and scratch removal across short early-film clips. [Source 1](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source 3](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source 4](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source 5](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Demo & examples

The paper and README show clean input, generated grayscale damage and a defect mask. They also report a blinded preference study. The study evaluates the authors’ experiment and does not prove recovery of original image content. [Source 1](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source 3](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source 4](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source 5](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Install

The project documents a locked uv environment, Python 3.11–3.13, and a separate step for the blue-noise basis and film textures. Read the dataset and texture terms before downloading inputs. [Source 1](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source 3](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source 4](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source 5](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. The project documents a locked uv environment, Python 3.11–3.13, and a separate step for the blue-noise basis and film textures. Read the dataset and texture terms before downloading inputs.

```sh
uv sync
```


```sh
uv run python scripts/degrade.py input_path=data/REDS/val_sharp output_path=data/out
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131) [Source 3](https://seungjunnah.github.io/Datasets/reds.html) [Source 4](https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/absolutedegradation/absolutecinema-real-scene-split-full) [Source 5](https://github.com/TytanMikJas/AbsoluteDegradation/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

1. Keep a clean reference and put clips in the documented folder-per-clip format. Start with a handful of lossless frames.
2. Generate an altered copy plus a soft spatial-defect mask using a fixed seed.
3. Inspect the masks and frame-to-frame artifacts before using them for training. Keep the held-out real film evaluation apart from synthetic pairs.

### Introduction

RestoraX combines a source CLI and a graph-based interface for comparing alternative restoration stages. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

### What it is good for

Comparing different restoration settings on a rights-cleared archival shot while retaining separate output references. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

### Demo & examples

The owner publishes sample restorations and a model-status matrix. The update provides branch previews and rejects geometry mismatches in the video writer. Independent production output comparison remains unverified. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

### Install

For a small test, use the project’s Python 3.11 environment, compatible PyTorch and the local CLI. The graph editor and API deployment have additional service and storage dependencies. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. For a small test, use the project’s Python 3.11 environment, compatible PyTorch and the local CLI. The graph editor and API deployment have additional service and storage dependencies.

```sh
conda create -n restorax python=3.11
```


```sh
restorax run --input old_film.mp4 --pipeline sr_x4
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

1. Use a short copy and select a single route designated functional in the current status table.
2. When using the graph, compare two branches on the same source fragment and check the delivered dimensions and audio.
3. Validate the selected codec, motion and continuity before creating a full derivative. Retain the untouched source.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

- **Hardware:** The available CPU and CUDA routes depend on the backend. The universal RAM, VRAM and disk needs of the complete suite remain undocumented.
- **Software:** Python 3.11 and PyTorch 2.3+ in the current README. The server workflow adds Celery and storage/database services. CUDA support and optional accelerators differ by model.
- **Platforms:** The source documents CPU and CUDA paths for selected backends. The optional external DLSS route and Linux compatibility have separate documented restrictions. No macOS full-stack support is inferred.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 5](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT applies to RestoraX. Real-ESRGAN and other downloaded weights, linked restorer projects, optional proprietary NVIDIA components and media have separate terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT applies to RestoraX. Real-ESRGAN and other downloaded weights, linked restorer projects, optional proprietary NVIDIA components and media have separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local model compute or own slot, weights and archive storage. Additional network services and external models have their own cost or access conditions.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

### Limitations

The status matrix identifies active models, classical fallbacks and adapters that remain unfinished. Choose one supported backend. Model previews and unit tests do not establish every advertised workflow. [Source 1](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/compare/de1acb3f0d1ad165e9c0094cbb9db5d9df184c7d...7ea090e15c7af64c7488178bd5fe0590ca448065) [Source 3](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/docs/samples.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/karailker/restorax/blob/main/restorax/models_catalog.py)

## AI Remaster Pipeline

Archival film restoration and preservation · VFX, compositing & relighting · Video, animation & film · Archives, media restoration & collections

Since the October 21 guide, recomposition takes account of vertical extensions and per-chunk offsets. The current implementation can join outpaint chunks at cuts or across overlaps and avoids mixing mismatched video codecs.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Comparing horizontal or vertical aspect-ratio extension for one short rights-cleared shot before deciding how much to use.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-19. No local execution implied.

Why Experimental: The maintainer explicitly describes the current pipeline as alpha. The selected output-composition workflow required recent fixes for mixed-codec stitches, segment offsets and vertically extended footage. https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code. Models, services and assets have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The source screenshots show the outpainting and recomposition interfaces. Actual scene quality, boundary continuity and accurate historical color remain unverified. [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** The official Windows setup provisions the environment, FFmpeg and managed ComfyUI. Review which models are needed for outpainting and recomposition before generating. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Apache-2.0 applies to the pipeline. Selected LTX, Wan, MiniMax, LoRA and color guidance artifacts have their own terms. Hosted RunPod or reference-image providers are separate. Local GPU compute or optional cloud services, plus checkpoints and working disk. Historical footage and generated-asset use have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. The current README identifies the software as alpha. Generated margin and lighting continuity need proofing, and colorization can invent detail. Keep the original clip.

### How it uses AI

ComfyUI model routes synthesize missing frame margins and can guide colorization. The compositor aligns that generated material with the source portion while retaining it as a separate layer. [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)

### Introduction

The application gives archival-video creators a stage-by-stage control surface for framing, color and final recomposition. [Source 1](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source 3](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)

### What it is good for

Comparing horizontal or vertical aspect-ratio extension for one short rights-cleared shot before deciding how much to use. [Source 1](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source 3](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)

The source screenshots show the outpainting and recomposition interfaces. Actual scene quality, boundary continuity and accurate historical color remain unverified. ### Install

The official Windows setup provisions the environment, FFmpeg and managed ComfyUI. Review which models are needed for outpainting and recomposition before generating. [Source 1](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source 3](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. The official Windows setup provisions the environment, FFmpeg and managed ComfyUI. Review which models are needed for outpainting and recomposition before generating.
### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/compare/0d68101a604a71f564b15b09be83f243c0ca6eef...8847c38ed18bd51552aa3864b7f5a0d55b8b89fd) [Source 3](https://github.com/dtaddis/ai-remaster-pipeline/blob/main/docs/final-composite.md)

1. Start with one short archival shot and record the original framing. Plan clear cut points or overlapping parts.
2. Generate the margins in small chunks and check how their offsets follow the original shot.
3. Preview the reassembled derivative and inspect seams and source-center alignment before considering a longer export.

- **Platforms:** Windows and NVIDIA are the documented path. Linux, macOS and other hardware support remain undocumented for the scoped local workflow.

## Kinocut

Video, animation & film · Editing, captions & post-production · AI agents for code-authored media production · Motion capture & character animation

This is the first complete guide in the library.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Turning parts of a source interview into separate reviewable clips and generating an editable caption track before a final assembly.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-19. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code. Models, services and assets have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The repository gives a trim, SRT and release checkpoint example. The checkpoint generates a storyboard and other QA artifacts. It does not validate editorial correctness or that the feature set is the same for all installs. [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the published 2.0.1 package with FFmpeg on PATH. Connect an MCP host only if you need an external agent, and enable optional tool groups deliberately. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** The application code is Apache-2.0. An external editor agent, transcription backend and optional models retain separate terms and possible charges. Local FFmpeg work does not require an API. Model-agent access, hosted transcription or other optional integrations may incur provider charges. [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. The documented checked commands reduce some editing errors. Kinocut itself does not serve as a sandbox. The unreleased video_direct route is outside the 2.0.1 guide.

### How it uses AI

Kinocut gives a connected model agent typed edit commands and optional AI transcription. It puts checks around FFmpeg calls and produces inspectable review artifacts for the resulting edit. [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)

### Introduction

Kinocut offers a CLI, Python client and MCP server for building auditable media edits in a local project. [Source 1](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source 3](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source 4](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)

### What it is good for

Turning parts of a source interview into separate reviewable clips and generating an editable caption track before a final assembly. [Source 1](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source 3](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source 4](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)

### Demo & examples

The repository gives a trim, SRT and release checkpoint example. The checkpoint generates a storyboard and other QA artifacts. It does not validate editorial correctness or that the feature set is the same for all installs. [Source 1](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source 3](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source 4](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)

### Install

Use the published 2.0.1 package with FFmpeg on PATH. Connect an MCP host only if you need an external agent, and enable optional tool groups deliberately. [Source 1](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source 3](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source 4](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use the published 2.0.1 package with FFmpeg on PATH. Connect an MCP host only if you need an external agent, and enable optional tool groups deliberately.

```sh
pip install kinocut==2.0.1
```


```sh
kino doctor
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://pypi.org/pypi/kinocut/json) [Source 3](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/pyproject.toml) [Source 4](https://github.com/KyaniteLabs/kinocut/blob/master/CHANGELOG.md)

1. Probe a short copy of a source clip and use the documented trim command on it.
2. Generate an SRT using the chosen transcription route. Correct names, timing and breaks in the text.
3. Use a release checkpoint to inspect frame thumbnails and the edit result before sharing.

### How it uses AI

The workbench uses Surya to identify page regions and recognize scanned text, and offers a separate model-assisted alt-text route. Writers can correct reading order before producing tagged text, Markdown, HTML or EPUB. ### Install

Install the documented Poppler system tools, set up the uv environment with the Surya extra, and launch the Workbench. Try the model route on representative pages before a book-scale job. uv sync --extra surya


```sh

```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/dallascard/pdf-converter/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/dallascard/pdf-converter/blob/main/pyproject.toml) [Source 3](https://github.com/VikParuchuri/surya) [Source 4](https://w3c.github.io/wai-website/media/av/description/)

1. Import scans and render or deskew the page images. Detect layout and correct bounding boxes and reading order.
2. Choose an OCR engine, inspect mixed scripts, tables and footnotes, and edit transcriptions.
3. Export a short EPUB or Markdown proof. Review image descriptions and navigation manually.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

- **Creative results · documented:** The authors publish dance clips and a physical foot-contact evaluation protocol. These do not establish the quality of choreography on a new song or clean motion on a different rig. [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/blob/main/download_model.sh) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/issues/53) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/issues/49)

Current first-hand issue reports leave checkpoint access and FBX conversion unresolved for some users. - **Models, services and costs · documented:** The MIT grant applies to this repository. The published checkpoint, AIST++ dataset, sample rig and imported music have separate terms. Review them before retraining or redistribution. Local GPU compute and storage, or the creator’s own rental environment. Dataset and rig terms remain separate. [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/blob/main/download_model.sh) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/issues/53) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/issues/49)

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. The maintainer says sustained updates are outside this research release’s scope. The rig and animation need retargeting and cleanup in a target DCC.

### How it uses AI

The released PyTorch model uses music conditioning and diffusion to synthesize a sequence of dance poses. The project describes exporting generated motion through an FBX route for Blender. [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/blob/main/download_model.sh) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/issues/53) [Source](https://github.com/Stanford-TML/EDGE/issues/49)

### Introduction

This research release links music cues to dance motion sequences and includes a path for reviewing them as character animation. Blocking dance animation to a short music cue before retargeting the motion onto an editable character. python test.py --music_dir custom_music/


2. Review the generated timing and ground contacts. Export a derivative when you want to animate in Blender.
3. Retarget and review the final performance on the production rig. Preserve the original generated sequence.

### How it uses AI

A trained text-conditioned flow-matching model generates human motion. Its guidance attempts to reduce jitter in motion built from text prompts. [Source](https://github.com/mccuba/FlowMotion/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

The published project includes separate HumanML3D and KIT-ML examples for text-to-motion research. [Source 1](https://github.com/mccuba/FlowMotion/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Testing written directions for animated character movement, then comparing the generated motion clips before manual blocking. [Source 1](https://github.com/mccuba/FlowMotion/blob/main/README.md)


2. Render the resulting sequence with the project’s own visualization route.

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. The guide reports a 720p input limit. Current issues describe garbled or unavailable feeds on some setups. Match the exact host, graphics routing and camera to the show.

### How it uses AI

MediaPipe vision models estimate face and hand landmarks, pose, and masks from a video input. The plugin exposes those predictions as TouchDesigner data and texture outputs for live scenes. [Source](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/releases/tag/v0.6.1) [Source](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/issues/183) [Source](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js) [Source](https://derivative.ca/community-post/asset/character-window-project-file/73399)

### Introduction

The plugin packages a model bridge and example components for inspecting vision data inside TouchDesigner. [Source 1](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/releases/tag/v0.6.1) [Source 3](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/issues/183) [Source 4](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js) [Source 5](https://derivative.ca/community-post/asset/character-window-project-file/73399)

### What it is good for

Using tracked hands to position a crop or changing a projected effect when someone enters a selected region. [Source 1](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/releases/tag/v0.6.1) [Source 3](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/issues/183) [Source 4](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js) [Source 5](https://derivative.ca/community-post/asset/character-window-project-file/73399)

### Demo & examples

The developer supplies a project and tutorial. A separate community artist published a Character Window project using its hand and face data. That older example establishes use of the approach, while the 0.6.1 capture path and current device reliability remain open. Keep the bundled .tox components together and use external component references as the guide describes. 2. Download the released plugin package and open the .toe example in the supported proprietary TouchDesigner host. Keep the bundled .tox components together and use external component references as the guide describes.
### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/releases/tag/v0.6.1) [Source 3](https://github.com/torinmb/mediapipe-touchdesigner/issues/183) [Source 4](https://ai.google.dev/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/pose_landmarker/web_js) [Source 5](https://derivative.ca/community-post/asset/character-window-project-file/73399)

1. Choose a camera that supports a documented input size. Enable only the hand or face vision task needed for the effect.
2. Read the corresponding data output and connect one coordinate to an effect. Add a defined fallback for missing detections.

## PDF Craft

Creative publishing & presentation · AI planner layouts, book arts and binding-aware publishing · Archives, media restoration & collections · Accessible media & assistive creation

This is the first complete guide in the library.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Making a proofable digital book from a cleared scan with headings, diagrams and mathematical notation.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-19. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code. Models, services and assets have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Official examples show Markdown and EPUB conversions from photographed or scanned pages. The project does not independently verify reading order or accuracy on your original. [Source](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/pdf-craft/json) [Source](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/pyproject.toml) [Source](https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-OCR/raw/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the PyPI package, install Poppler, and configure a specific remote OCR endpoint or the documented local NVIDIA path. Review model and data handling before selecting the route. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/pdf-craft/json) [Source](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/pyproject.toml) [Source](https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-OCR/raw/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT applies to the library. DeepSeek OCR and other named routes are separate downloaded or remote models. A remote service receives document page data. Refer to its own model grant and data policy. Local routes use local compute and storage. Remote OCR, model-based translation and the hosted converter can be separately billed. [Source](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://pypi.org/pypi/pdf-craft/json) [Source](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/pyproject.toml) [Source](https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-OCR/raw/main/README.md)

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. Manually proof tables, equations, outlines and accessibility. The hosted converter and the installable library have separate operational terms. A captured package listing does not verify every supplied model.

### How it uses AI

The library applies a configurable OCR model such as DeepSeek OCR or another supported endpoint to page images, then reconstructs the text and images for editable Markdown or EPUB. ### Introduction

This library focuses on recovering useful document structure from scanned books and technical PDFs. 2. Use the PyPI package, install Poppler, and configure a specific remote OCR endpoint or the documented local NVIDIA path. Review model and data handling before selecting the route.

```sh
python -m pip install pdf-craft
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://pypi.org/pypi/pdf-craft/json) [Source 3](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft/blob/main/pyproject.toml) [Source 4](https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-OCR/raw/main/README.md)

1. Start with representative pages that contain headings, captions and tables. Pick the documented local or remote OCR configuration.
2. Convert to Markdown first and correct headings, image placement, symbols and reading order.
3. Export EPUB and check navigation and resizing with the intended reader. Keep the original scans.

## AiSRT

Editing, captions & post-production · Accessible media & assistive creation · Video, animation & film

This completes an earlier pending guide in the library. The current source review is dated 2026-11-19.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Drafting and correcting captions for a recorded interview or work-in-progress cut, particularly when the artist wants the original media to remain local.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-19. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code. Models, services and assets have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The maintainer shows the Chinese desktop interface and its caption preview. The capture does not establish accuracy and timing on live performance audio or multilingual recordings. [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Follow the English or Chinese source setup and make FFmpeg plus ffprobe visible on PATH. Select the speech and translation settings and allow the first-run model download where required. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT applies to the desktop and Python source. Speech, translation and optional model revisions have separate terms and can require a first-run download. Local compute, storage and model downloads. Project documentation does not establish a required metered API for the local workflow. [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

This assessment is based on published sources. Hands-on verification remains pending. A local route keeps input files in the chosen environment, but language and speaker changes still demand review. Python, FFmpeg and model files are not prebundled in the lightweight portable Windows package.

### How it uses AI

The tool transcribes local audio or video with learned speech recognition and can pass the text through a separate local translation model. It writes editable SRT and provides a review screen. [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Introduction

AiSRT is a Mandarin-documented subtitle workspace with a local edit preview and separate transcription and translation settings. [Source 1](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### What it is good for

Drafting and correcting captions for a recorded interview or work-in-progress cut, particularly when the artist wants the original media to remain local. [Source 1](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Demo & examples

The maintainer shows the Chinese desktop interface and its caption preview. The capture does not establish accuracy and timing on live performance audio or multilingual recordings. [Source 1](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Install

Follow the English or Chinese source setup and make FFmpeg plus ffprobe visible on PATH. Select the speech and translation settings and allow the first-run model download where required. [Source 1](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Follow the English or Chinese source setup and make FFmpeg plus ffprobe visible on PATH. Select the speech and translation settings and allow the first-run model download where required.

```sh
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/README.en.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/zkwi/AiSRT/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

1. Use a short clip with clear speaker changes. Choose the source language and whether to run local translation.
2. Generate the SRT, then check speaker names, line length and each time range in the preview.
3. Export a copy of the corrected captions. Check synchronization after importing it into the final editor.
